# The Swinging Door - studio report, 2026-09-06

Built for Rick. Every image the studio generated today, in the order it happened, with the ask that drove it, the thinking behind the decision, the exact prompt and settings, and the verdict. Images are in `images/`, prompts in `prompts/`.

## 001. figure-drew-02-toward render seed 7 laid in full plate

![figure-drew-02-toward render seed 7 laid in full plate](images/001-figure-drew-02-toward-render-seed-7-laid-in-full-plate.png)

**The thinking.** Rendered in context - the plate as it stands behind this part, with only this part's own silhouette blocked in as values - so the pen, light and perspective are inherited; only the mask is laid back. Part note: DREW-02 TURNED TO BARCLAY, HEAD UP - the anchor pose. Drew SEATED IN THE LEFT-HAND CHAIR AT THE MARBLE COUNTER, SEEN FROM BEHIND AND SLIGHTLY TO HIS RIGHT - body turned 146 degrees from the lens so his right shoulder is toward us and the chair back stands in front of him - HIS HEAD TURNED BACK AND ACROSS TO BARCLAY IN THE RIGHT-HAND CHAIR: head yaw 118 degrees, and THE HEAD IS CARRIED UP, the line from the centre of his eye to the black tip of his bill lying 42 degrees below horizontal. His eye slides sideways at Barclay's face; the bill is CLOSED, a slender even-taper wedge whose BLACK OUTER THIRD IS THE OUTLINE with one bright highlight ribbon inside it. One neck reversal, the head carried forward of where the neck left the body. Near hand on the martini stem, at least three digits closed on it and one crossing in front. Shoulders 1.32 m, crown 1.65 m on a 0.76 m seat. Cropped at the counter: chest-up, no legs, no stools. He never looks at us.

## 005. Drew, anchor pose, round 2: garment-tone vest, bare-bird negatives, wardrobe sheet as a third reference - still undressed on all four seeds

![Drew, anchor pose, round 2: garment-tone vest, bare-bird negatives, wardrobe sheet as a third reference - still undressed on all four seeds](images/005-drew-anchor-pose-round-2-garment-tone-vest-bare-bird-negatives-wardrobe-sheet-as-a-third-reference-still-undre.png)

**The ask.** make sure the characters are huggable have beautiful texture for their species

**The thinking.** A darker vest tone in the block-in and a wardrobe sheet as a reference did not hold the garment: from behind, a mid tone on a bird's back is plumage to the model. The neck and the plumage strokes are right on seeds 7 and 33. The fix follows the room's own lesson - texture is drawn, not asked for: the vest carries a knit lattice and ribbed armhole bands in the block-in, and the from-behind staging study the founder scored highest (canon/vision/studies/duo-behind.png: Drew in his vest and collar over his shoulder, Barclay beside him) becomes the primary reference.

**Settings.** figure-drew-02-toward, seeds 7/21/33/44, refs bust+head+wardrobe-sheet, vest 104-128

**Verdict.** rejected - round three with a drawn knit and the duo-behind study

*Logged 00:09.*

## 010. Drew, anchor pose, round 3: knit lattice drawn into the vest, the from-behind study as reference - still a bare bird; the lattice came back as scales

![Drew, anchor pose, round 3: knit lattice drawn into the vest, the from-behind study as reference - still a bare bird; the lattice came back as scales](images/010-drew-anchor-pose-round-3-knit-lattice-drawn-into-the-vest-the-from-behind-study-as-reference-still-a-bare-bird.png)

**The ask.** make sure the characters are huggable have beautiful texture for their species

**The thinking.** Twelve renders over three rounds say the whole-frame route cannot hold Drew's clothing from behind: he is a quarter of the frame, a bow tie is a few pixels, and the prompt only ever described Picture 1, so the reference pictures were treated as style. Two changes for round four, both in the assembler: the prompt now names the reference pictures as the character's own art to copy (identity, texture and clothing), and a figure renders from a 2:3 crop of the frame at the model's full resolution - four times the pixels on him - then scales back into place for laying.

**Settings.** figure-drew-02-toward seeds 7/21/33/44; refs bust+head+duo-behind; vest lattice 9 px at 62 with ribbed armholes

**Verdict.** rejected - round four as a detail render

*Logged 00:23.*

## 020. Drew's head, rendered on its own from a tight crop with the head tile leading: seed 101 is the first proper Drew head - and why the composite showed a dark block beside it

![Drew's head, rendered on its own from a tight crop with the head tile leading: seed 101 is the first proper Drew head - and why the composite showed a dark block beside it](images/020-drew-s-head-rendered-on-its-own-from-a-tight-crop-with-the-head-tile-leading-seed-101-is-the-first-proper-drew.png)

**The ask.** the eyes must look human and huggable ... great texture unique to their animal

**The thinking.** A head-only render at four times the scale, with the head tile as the first reference, gives a real Drew head: the heavy bill bending down to its black tip, the lidded eye with a visible white, plumage in fine strokes. Composited onto the seed-7 body through the figure's own silhouette, a dark block appeared beside the head: the block-in's bill was a long thin wedge (the bible's swan proportions), so the silhouette reached past every rendered bill into the head render's background. The approved plates and the published set both carry the heavier bill, so the block-in's bill is now deeper and bent down to match, and the bodies and heads are re-rendered against it. Decision carried to the founder: the bible's swan wording versus the plates' heavy bill.

**Settings.** head crop 280,740,560,1160 at 1184x1776, refs head+bust+duo-behind, seeds 101-104; combine_heads.py through mask x head window; bill depth 0.46 L, tip down 2.2 cm

**Verdict.** head route works - composite geometry being aligned

*Logged 00:54.*

## 026. Drew, round 5: body (knit vest) plus head renders composited through the silhouette - the head is right on seed 104, but the head render's own background leaks wherever the rendered head sits smaller than the block-in

![Drew, round 5: body (knit vest) plus head renders composited through the silhouette - the head is right on seed 104, but the head render's own background leaks wherever the rendered head sits smaller than the block-in](images/026-drew-round-5-body-knit-vest-plus-head-renders-composited-through-the-silhouette-the-head-is-right-on-seed-104-.png)

**The ask.** the eyes must look human and huggable

**The thinking.** With the deeper bill in the block-in, the body renders keep the vest and the head renders give a real Drew head (seed 104: heavy bill to a black tip, lidded eye, fine plumage). But a composite cut by the block-in's silhouette still shows the head render's re-imagined background wherever the rendered head is smaller or offset, and a mask taken from a render is unreliable in engraving line. Round six closes the loop without compositing: the good head render becomes the FIRST reference for the body render, so the body render draws that head at the block-in's position.

**Settings.** bodies s7/s44 crop 60,700,700,1660; heads s101/102/104 crop 280,740,560,1160; combine_heads.py window 300,815,540,985 x mask

**Verdict.** head identity found; composite retired in favour of the head-as-reference loop

*Logged 01:00.*

## 032. Drew character study, round 1, seed 41 - the study rig proves out, the bill does not

![Drew character study, round 1, seed 41 - the study rig proves out, the bill does not](images/032-drew-character-study-round-1-seed-41-the-study-rig-proves-out-the-bill-does-not.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Last night's cast renders came back as swans and geese because they were drawn by room-part.py on sensenova-u1.5 from crude block-ins. First: rebuild the study rig on the HOUSE recipe - the model and prompt that made the plates Rick approved - and prove it end to end on Drew.

**The thinking.** The approved plates were never drawn from block-ins. They were drawn by local/qwen-image-edit-2511 through lib/generate.ts: an EDIT model handed a finished picture as Picture 1, the character's own portrait tile after it, and a short numbered list of changes, at the Lightning 8-step cfg-1 setting recorded in canon/vision/studies/duo-behind.txt (seed 41). So scripts/cast-study.py reproduces that recipe in Python for one character at a time. Picture 1 is a chest-up crop of Drew alone cut straight out of canon/plates/duo.png - the plate Rick APPROVED - so the model has nothing to invent about the camera, the crop or the room; Picture 2 is canon/vision/studies/drew.png, the same portrait tile VISION_REFS sends. The prompt is localPrompt's own shape: the Picture roster, the numbered EDITS (screen off, slate wiped, bottles filled and unlettered, redraw the character to Picture 2, the business, everything-else-stays), then the LOCAL fence of canon/MASTER-PROMPT.md re-pointed from two seated gentlemen to one - by exact-string replacement into canon's own sentences, with every miss recorded in the sidecar so a canon rewrite shows up as data instead of quietly drawing the duo again.

**Settings.** scripts/cast-study.py --character drew --seed 41 | local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST 127.0.0.1:8000/api/generate | fast Lightning: 8 steps, cfg 1, euler/simple, shift 3.0 | 4:5 -> 1344x1680 | 2 references (canon/plates/duo.png crop 140,540,640,800; canon/vision/studies/drew.png), grayscaled, normalised, capped at 1024 wide, JPEG q90, inline data URIs | LOCAL_NEGATIVE verbatim | 46.8s

**Prompt.** [prompts/032-drew-character-study-round-1-seed-41-the-study-rig-proves-out-the-bill-does-not.prompt.txt](prompts/032-drew-character-study-round-1-seed-41-the-study-rig-proves-out-the-bill-does-not.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** RIG PASSES, CHARACTER FAILS. The rig is right: one figure, no second bird, the engraved pen and the full tonal range held, every bottle label blank, no stray lettering on the shelf, the bow tie, collar band, knitted vest and feathered hand all present, the martini and nut bowl on the marble. Drew himself is a REJECT and the founder's word is fair. (1) THE BILL is the fatal fault - heavy, deeply hooked, curling to a point below the jaw with the black filling the whole lower half. A flamingo's bill is shallow, kinked once and downturned only at the end, with the black on the OUTER THIRD. This reads vulture, not flamingo. (2) THE NECK has no S-curve: it is thick and short, and the head sits on it as a bulb. The one reversal the founder asked for is absent. (3) THE PLUMAGE is drawn in scale-like leaf strokes, not the fine dash-strokes of the house line. (4) The camera crept much closer than Picture 1's crop, so the head fills the frame and the sweater vest is half out of it. (5) Two small strays: a fragment of mirrored window lettering at top-left and an extra glass at bottom-right. What IS right and must be kept: the eye - heavy-lidded, visible white, dark iris with a catchlight, warm and human. Round 2 must attack the bill and the neck by name in the EDITS and pull the camera back.

*Logged 08:48.*

## 033. Drew round 1 seed 41 the room comes back the bill still hooks

![Drew round 1 seed 41 the room comes back the bill still hooks](images/033-drew-round-1-seed-41-the-room-comes-back-the-bill-still-hooks.png)

**The ask.** The founder: "its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character." Round 1 of the Drew perfecting loop: make him read as a FLAMINGO — the S-curved neck with one reversal, the shallow downturned bill with the black OUTER THIRD, plumage in fine dash-strokes, the heavy-lidded human eye — in the knitted vest, collar and black bow tie, at the marble.

**The thinking.** The rig test (image 032) proved the pipeline and failed the bird, and reading the two references side by side said why: Picture 1 was the fault. The crop of the approved plate duo.png that the study feeds the model as 'this IS the picture being edited' ITSELF draws a deep scimitar bill with black filling the lower jaw, a thick straight neck and scale-like plumage — and it puts that wrong head at full size exactly where the model must draw a head. The identity tile canon/vision/studies/drew.png is the one that is right. So the model was obeying us. Three changes this round, no code rewrite of the recipe: (1) RE-POINTED PICTURE 1 from box (140,540,640,800) to (40,500,780,975) — a wider, further-back crop that carries the whole room (window and its mirrored name, both back-bar shelves, marble, martini, nut bowl, chair) and shrinks the wrong head to a fraction of the frame, which also answers the round-0 fault of the camera creeping in; (2) ADDED A THIRD REFERENCE — a large close cut of the CORRECT head, bill and neck from drew.png (box 250,30,560,600) — so the bill has an authority at head scale, and relabelled Picture 1 to say in words that it is the authority on the ROOM ONLY and that its bird's hooked black-jawed bill is the fault being corrected; (3) NAMED THE THREE FAULTS AS AN EDIT LINE — the bill shallow with ONE kink and solid black on the OUTER THIRD only and never curling below the jaw, the neck an S with ONE reversal, the plumage rows of fine short dash-strokes — plus a pose line pinning the camera at Picture 1's distance. cast-study.py gained --picture1-box, --picture1-label and --ref to do this without editing canon. Six seeds on the house recipe.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511, provider local, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps cfg 1.0 euler/simple shift 3.0, 3 references (Picture 1 = canon/plates/duo.png box 40,500,780,975; Picture 2 = canon/vision/studies/drew.png; Picture 3 = drew.png box 250,30,560,600), LOCAL_NEGATIVE, tag study-drew-r1, seeds 41/7/21/33/44/55, ~47 s each. Zero canon-drift misses. scripts/cast-study.py

**Prompt.** [prompts/033-drew-round-1-seed-41-the-room-comes-back-the-bill-still-hooks.prompt.txt](prompts/033-drew-round-1-seed-41-the-room-comes-back-the-bill-still-hooks.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** REJECT on the bill, PASS on the rig. The wider Picture 1 worked: the camera is back at the plate's distance, the window carries a clean mirrored THE SWINGING DOOR, both back-bar shelves, the marble, the martini and the nut bowl are all in frame, and the neck now has a real S with one reversal. But the bill is still the round-0 fault at three-quarter strength — the black is a long wedge down the lower mandible curling to a point below the jaw, not a cap on the outer third. Plumage still reads as leaf-shaped scales, not dash-strokes. Bottle labels carry pseudo-marks instead of blank paper.

*Logged 08:59.*

## 038. Drew round 1 seed 55 the best flamingo of the round

![Drew round 1 seed 55 the best flamingo of the round](images/038-drew-round-1-seed-55-the-best-flamingo-of-the-round.png)

**The ask.** The founder: "its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character." Round 1 of the Drew perfecting loop: make him read as a FLAMINGO — the S-curved neck with one reversal, the shallow downturned bill with the black OUTER THIRD, plumage in fine dash-strokes, the heavy-lidded human eye — in the knitted vest, collar and black bow tie, at the marble.

**The thinking.** The rig test (image 032) proved the pipeline and failed the bird, and reading the two references side by side said why: Picture 1 was the fault. The crop of the approved plate duo.png that the study feeds the model as 'this IS the picture being edited' ITSELF draws a deep scimitar bill with black filling the lower jaw, a thick straight neck and scale-like plumage — and it puts that wrong head at full size exactly where the model must draw a head. The identity tile canon/vision/studies/drew.png is the one that is right. So the model was obeying us. Three changes this round, no code rewrite of the recipe: (1) RE-POINTED PICTURE 1 from box (140,540,640,800) to (40,500,780,975) — a wider, further-back crop that carries the whole room (window and its mirrored name, both back-bar shelves, marble, martini, nut bowl, chair) and shrinks the wrong head to a fraction of the frame, which also answers the round-0 fault of the camera creeping in; (2) ADDED A THIRD REFERENCE — a large close cut of the CORRECT head, bill and neck from drew.png (box 250,30,560,600) — so the bill has an authority at head scale, and relabelled Picture 1 to say in words that it is the authority on the ROOM ONLY and that its bird's hooked black-jawed bill is the fault being corrected; (3) NAMED THE THREE FAULTS AS AN EDIT LINE — the bill shallow with ONE kink and solid black on the OUTER THIRD only and never curling below the jaw, the neck an S with ONE reversal, the plumage rows of fine short dash-strokes — plus a pose line pinning the camera at Picture 1's distance. cast-study.py gained --picture1-box, --picture1-label and --ref to do this without editing canon. Six seeds on the house recipe.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511, provider local, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps cfg 1.0 euler/simple shift 3.0, 3 references (Picture 1 = canon/plates/duo.png box 40,500,780,975; Picture 2 = canon/vision/studies/drew.png; Picture 3 = drew.png box 250,30,560,600), LOCAL_NEGATIVE, tag study-drew-r1, seeds 41/7/21/33/44/55, ~47 s each. Zero canon-drift misses. scripts/cast-study.py

**Prompt.** [prompts/038-drew-round-1-seed-55-the-best-flamingo-of-the-round.prompt.txt](prompts/038-drew-round-1-seed-55-the-best-flamingo-of-the-round.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** BEST OF THE ROUND, still a reject against the founder's bar. Closest to a real flamingo: the longest pale upper mandible, the shallowest downturn, the black wedge starting furthest out and the least hook of the six; the neck reads as a true S with one reversal, leaning back off the shoulders and forward into the head; the eye is exactly right — heavy-lidded, visible white, dark iris, one catchlight, warm and unhurried; the arm runs unbroken feathered from the vest armhole to the hand with NO cuff; collar band, black bow tie, ribbed V-neck vest, martini, nut bowl and mirrored window name all correct. What still fails: the black is roughly the outer 40-45 percent and still tips just below the jaw rather than capping the outer third, and the plumage is still shingled leaf-plates instead of rows of fine dash-strokes.

*Logged 08:59.*

## 040. Drew round 3 - the root-cause fix: a composited Picture 1 with the correct head pasted on

![Drew round 3 - the root-cause fix: a composited Picture 1 with the correct head pasted on](images/040-drew-round-3-the-root-cause-fix-a-composited-picture-1-with-the-correct-head-pasted-on.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 2's judges found six seeds out of six still drawing a hooked, black-jawed bill and a hunched neck, and concluded the prose was losing to the picture.

**The thinking.** Qwen-image-edit copies Picture 1's silhouette. Picture 1 was a crop of the approved plate, and that crop CONTAINS all three faults - the hooked bill with black filling the lower half, the thick J-neck, the scale-like plumage - so every seed copied them and no amount of wording won. So Picture 1 was rebuilt in PIL before the call (scripts/make-drew-picture1.py): the plate still supplies the room, the camera, the crop and the light, but Drew's head, bill and S-neck are cut out of the official portrait canon/vision/studies/drew.png as a filled silhouette, scaled to 0.72, and pasted onto his collar; the old head is painted out with clean back bar copied from further right on the same plate. The seam is left crude on purpose - the edit now has to blend a correct head instead of inventing one.

**Settings.** PIL/scipy composite, no model call. Plate crop duo.png @ 40,500,780x975; head cut drew.png @ 170,30-830,660; scale 0.72, offset (10,105); erase 108,118-482,502 covered from +300px right.

**Verdict.** Works. The bill has one clean kink with the black cap on the outer third, the neck is a true S with one reversal and the head is carried high with daylight under the jaw. This one change fixed the neck in ALL SIX renders that followed.

*Logged 09:20.*

## 041. Drew round 3 seed 55 - the best flamingo yet: right bill, right neck, knitted vest, human hands

![Drew round 3 seed 55 - the best flamingo yet: right bill, right neck, knitted vest, human hands](images/041-drew-round-3-seed-55-the-best-flamingo-yet-right-bill-right-neck-knitted-vest-human-hands.png)

**The ask.** Perfect Drew: the S-curved neck with ONE reversal, the heavy downturned bill with the black outer third, the knitted sweater vest over a collared shirt with the small black bow tie, the heavy-lidded human eye, friendly and professional.

**The thinking.** Composited Picture 1 (the correct head already on the plate), Picture 2 the official portrait, and a NEW Picture 3: a bill-only tile cut at drew.png 520,130,310x390 - the eye and the whole bill, so the black cap reads as the authority instead of being a fraction of a mostly-neck tile. Seven new numbered EDITs off the judges' notes: the bill (one abrupt bend two thirds along, blunt not hooked, depth under a third of the length, black capping BOTH mandibles, nostril a thin slit), the S-neck, a turn toward the camera, the knitted wardrobe, the four-fingered feathered hands, the counter and props, and the blank labels.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST /api/generate, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps cfg 1.0 euler/simple shift 3.0, 3 references, seed 55, 48.6s. Prompt variant A.

**Verdict.** The best Drew the studio has drawn. PASS on every character fault the founder named: the bill is a flamingo's - straight and pale for two thirds, one clean kink, a blunt downturn, the solid black a cap over the outer third covering both mandibles and wrapping the tip, no black jaw stripe, no hook. The neck is a true S with one reversal, slim, head carried high. The vest is genuinely KNITTED with a ribbed V band, ribbed armholes and visible stitches over a collared shirt with placket, buttons and the bow tie KNOT facing us. The hands have four fingers and an opposed thumb, no claws. The eye is warm and huggable. FAILS on staging, not on character: the turn-to-camera edit pulled him off his stool so the marble counter has left the picture, he holds the martini instead of resting his hand on the slab, the belt and trousers show, one stray rocks glass remains at frame-right, and the arm plumage is still leaf-shaped rather than dash-strokes.

*Logged 09:20.*

## 042. Drew round 3 contact sheet - six seeds against the old kit tiles, and the staging-versus-character trade

![Drew round 3 contact sheet - six seeds against the old kit tiles, and the staging-versus-character trade](images/042-drew-round-3-contact-sheet-six-seeds-against-the-old-kit-tiles-and-the-staging-versus-character-trade.png)

**The ask.** Render six seeds for Drew, look at every one, name the best, and show them beside the kit's bust and head tiles.

**The thinking.** Seeds 55 and 21 ran prompt variant A. Seed 41 came back as three figures on a sketchbook page, so variant B dropped the words 'paste-up', 'cut in', 'seam' and 'unfinished' from Picture 1's label - that language was inviting a collage - and bolted him back to the marble; 41 and 7 were re-shot on it. But B told the model Picture 1 was FINAL, so it copied the plate wholesale and the bill drifted heron-ish again. Variant C (33, 44) put A's forceful turn and an ACTIVE 'redraw the bill from Picture 3' back on top of B's counter anchor.

**Settings.** Six renders at 420px beside canon/characters/flamingo/kit bust.png and head.png. All six: local/qwen-image-edit-2511, 4:5 1344x1680, fast 8-step cfg 1.0, 3 references, ~49s each.

**Verdict.** BEST IS SEED 55. Read against the two kit tiles frame-left - which still carry the old hooked black-jawed bill and the hunched neck - all six new renders have a proper S-neck, so the composited Picture 1 fixed that outright. The bill only comes out right when the model is pushed to REDRAW the character (variant A, seeds 55 and 21); whenever the prompt protects Picture 1's staging (B and C, seeds 41/7/33/44) the room comes back correctly but the bill re-absorbs the plate's hook. Seed 44 grew a human bartender and seed 41's first take grew two extra figures - the negative prompt cannot catch that, because at cfg 1.0 the fast Lightning pass never reads it. Staging and character are in direct competition at 8 steps and the next round has to settle it.

*Logged 09:20.*

## 043. Drew round 4 reference fix 1 - the bill built as a flat two-tone diagram

![Drew round 4 reference fix 1 - the bill built as a flat two-tone diagram](images/043-drew-round-4-reference-fix-1-the-bill-built-as-a-flat-two-tone-diagram.png)

**The ask.** Round 4 of perfecting DREW. The judges' fixes after round 3: re-cut Picture 2 to head-and-shoulders so the belt, the trousers and the hand on the martini stem cannot be copied back out of it; build a flat two-tone bill DIAGRAM instead of using a photo-crop as the bill authority; repaint the bill in the composited Picture 1 and delete the sentence that was protecting the old one; re-cut kit/bust.png and kit/head.png; replace the black-cap, bill-shape, bill-size, eye, plumage and staging paragraphs; add the new negatives; split the work into a staging pass and a narrow bill-repair pass; carry seeds 55 and 21, drop 41, 7, 33 and 44.

**The thinking.** The judges ordered a DIAGRAM rather than a photo-crop as the bill authority, because a diagram cannot be misread. scripts/make-drew-bill.py holds the geometry once: a pale shank dead straight for two thirds, ONE sharp downward corner, then straight to a blunt rounded end, with a single vertical line crossing the WHOLE bill at that corner and everything outboard of it solid black together - top ridge, both sides, lower jaw and wrapped tip. Nothing is lettered on it: the model letters whatever it is shown. The same geometry drives the repaint of the portrait, so the diagram and the tiles cannot drift.

**Settings.** Drawn in PIL, no model. Geometry: eye-to-tip 168 px against a 166 px skull; depth at the corner 42 px, under a third of eye-to-tip.

**Verdict.** Built as ordered. NOT USED AS A REFERENCE IN THE END - see the render below where the model drew it as an object in the room.

*Logged 10:09.*

## 060. Drew round 4B the rebuilt Picture 1 the reference that fixed the bill

![Drew round 4B the rebuilt Picture 1 the reference that fixed the bill](images/060-drew-round-4b-the-rebuilt-picture-1-the-reference-that-fixed-the-bill.png)

**The ask.** The judges: rebuild Picture 1 without the white ghost-bill mask, do not mask the bill with a hard-edged flat shape, and fix its neck - it is a J-hook with the head at shoulder level and the renders copy it rather than the neck EDIT.

**The thinking.** Round 4 pass 1's Picture 1 took its head from kit/portrait-billfixed.png, a RETOUCHED portrait whose bill had been painted over as a flat two-tone bent stick, with a hard-edged white mask laid over the bill region. An edit model copies a flat vector shape as a flat vector shape, and six seeds proved it. This one (scripts/make-drew-picture1-round4b.py) cuts the head straight out of the UNRETOUCHED canon/vision/studies/drew.png - which already carries the correct deep, once-bent, black-tipped flamingo bill - pastes it at scale .74 and dy 75 so the head is carried above the shoulder line instead of hooking down to it, feathers the silhouette into the painted-out back bar so there is no straight edge anywhere, and paints the second rocks glass off the marble. A neck-lengthening variant was built and rejected: stretching the neck band broke the silhouette fill and left dark halo artefacts down its left side.

**Settings.** Built from canon/plates/duo.png crop (40,500,780x975) plus the head cut (170,30,860,660) of canon/vision/studies/drew.png; PIL/scipy silhouette fill, feather 2.5, bottom fade 60 px. 780x975, greyscale.

**Verdict.** This is the change that moved the bill. Nothing else about the recipe changed - same model, same fast 8-step cfg 1 pass, same six seeds - and the bill came right on every one of the six. Remaining weakness carried by this same tile: the portrait's own plumage is drawn in closed leaf and teardrop plates, so every render inherits them, and the neck is one reversal rather than the two the judges asked for.

*Logged 10:25.*

---

Expression overall reads deadpan-stern, not amiable (founder gate 7).

**The thinking.** Not room-part.py and not sensenova — the house model local/qwen-image-edit-2511 through scripts/cast-study.py, the recipe Rick's approved plates were made with. Picture 1 is a 4:5 crop of the approved duo.png with Barclay alone, RE-CUT this round to (640,620,560,700) — wider and lower than the script's default (680,640,520,650) — because Drew's round 1 showed the model pushing in past Picture 1's crop; a roomier Picture 1 keeps the shoulders, the collar V and the jacket front in frame. Picture 2 is his official portrait; Picture 3 is NEW this round: canon/characters/dog/kit/head.png, the tight head tile, because his look card's failure mode 2 is that the black lip band and the eye's paper-white are the first two marks the pipeline loses, and that tile carries both at native resolution. Three references is exactly the qwen family's max_refs, so nothing is dropped. Five extra EDIT lines name, positively, what his judge checklist fails him on: the sad first glance (worry in the brows only, mouth closed with the corner hooking UP), the three-mark eye and the black-labrador lip line, the layered coat with three arcing rows of freckles and a modest ruff, and the wardrobe/hands/no tail, plus a hold-the-camera line.

He is unmistakably a golden retriever now — layered fur in individual strokes, drop ear with fringe, black nose the blackest mark, mouth closed with the corner hooking up, the eye carrying paper-white and one catchlight. Two faults: the camera pushed past Picture 1's crop so the head fills the frame and the jacket is half out of it, and the bottle labels came back with scribbled pseudo-lettering instead of the blank panels edit 3 asked for. ## 063. Barclay character study round 1 seed 7 - the warmest face of the round

![Barclay character study round 1 seed 7 - the warmest face of the round](images/063-barclay-character-study-round-1-seed-7-the-warmest-face-of-the-round.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Barclay's turn — draw him as a golden retriever actually drawn like one, warm and professional, on the house model, six seeds, and name the best.

**The thinking.** Not room-part.py and not sensenova — the house model local/qwen-image-edit-2511 through scripts/cast-study.py, the recipe Rick's approved plates were made with. Picture 1 is a 4:5 crop of the approved duo.png with Barclay alone, RE-CUT this round to (640,620,560,700) — wider and lower than the script's default (680,640,520,650) — because Drew's round 1 showed the model pushing in past Picture 1's crop; a roomier Picture 1 keeps the shoulders, the collar V and the jacket front in frame. Picture 2 is his official portrait; Picture 3 is NEW this round: canon/characters/dog/kit/head.png, the tight head tile, because his look card's failure mode 2 is that the black lip band and the eye's paper-white are the first two marks the pipeline loses, and that tile carries both at native resolution. Three references is exactly the qwen family's max_refs, so nothing is dropped. Five extra EDIT lines name, positively, what his judge checklist fails him on: the sad first glance (worry in the brows only, mouth closed with the corner hooking UP), the three-mark eye and the black-labrador lip line, the layered coat with three arcing rows of freckles and a modest ruff, and the wardrobe/hands/no tail, plus a hold-the-camera line.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, 3 references (duo.png crop 640,620,560,700 + vision/studies/barclay.png + characters/dog/kit/head.png), scripts/cast-study.py, seed 7

**Prompt.** [prompts/063-barclay-character-study-round-1-seed-7-the-warmest-face-of-the-round.prompt.txt](prompts/063-barclay-character-study-round-1-seed-7-the-warmest-face-of-the-round.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** KEEP AS THE FACE REFERENCE. The best expression in the six: both inner brows lifted with one faint forehead crease, the closed lip line hooking clearly UP at the corner, cheeks full, the eye's dark mass at the front of the opening with paper-white behind it and a single catchlight, ten-odd freckles in loose rows. Ear fringe is fine, not wiry. Faults: the flag pin came back as a blank white triangle with no canton (checklist 12 fail), the lip band is grey and stops short of the eye's front corner, and two bottle labels still carry pseudo-text. Second place.

*Logged 10:41.*

Remaining faults for round 2: the black lip band is a grey line that stops short of the eye's front corner instead of a fine true-black line ending under it; the freckles run six to ten, under the fifteen to twenty in three rows; a couple of bottle labels still carry faint pseudo-marks.

*Logged 10:41.*

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, 3 references (duo.png crop 640,620,560,700 + vision/studies/barclay.png + characters/dog/kit/head.png), scripts/cast-study.py, seeds 41 / 7 / 21 / 33 / 44 / 55

**Prompt.** [prompts/068-barclay-character-study-round-1-the-contact-sheet-six-seeds-against-the-reference-kit.prompt.txt](prompts/068-barclay-character-study-round-1-the-contact-sheet-six-seeds-against-the-reference-kit.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** Round 1 is a pass on the SPECIES and a hold on the DETAIL. Six for six he is now a golden retriever drawn like one — layered fur in individual strokes, drop ear with fringe, black nose, closed mouth with the corner turning up, the human eye with visible white and a catchlight — which is the thing the founder said was missing. SEED 44 IS THE PICK: it alone holds Picture 1's camera AND draws the flag pin properly on the lapel with a legible canton. Seed 7 is the warmest face and seed 55 is third; seed 33 is a reject (a second dog hallucinated on the back-bar shelf); seeds 41 and 21 pushed the camera in past the plate's crop. Round 2 should carry seed 44 forward with three fixes: make the black lip band a fine TRUE-BLACK line running two nose-widths to directly under the front corner of the near eye, raise the muzzle freckles to fifteen to twenty in three arcing rows, and kill the last pseudo-lettering on the bottle labels.

*Logged 10:41.*

## 069. Barclay character study round 2 seed 41 the head turns but the pick returns

![Barclay character study round 2 seed 41 the head turns but the pick returns](images/069-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-41-the-head-turns-but-the-pick-returns.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 2 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-1 list: give him his own signature prop (a rocks glass with one large ice cube and one stemless cherry, never a pick), turn his head and both eyes forward into the room toward Drew, draw a legible USA flag pin on the near lapel, run the true-black lip line the full two nose-widths and hook it up, keep the ear a leather of fine individual strokes, pull the camera back, and keep every second dog, every scribbled label and every stray cheek contour out of the picture.

**The thinking.** The judges named two reference changes as the root causes, and looking at the plates at full size only one of them held. (1) They asked for Picture 1 to be re-cut from canon/plates/duo-barclay.png because duo.png has him 'already near-profile'. Cropped side by side the two plates draw the SAME near-profile head - and so do all twelve approved showcase plates; there is no picture in canon of a forward-turned Barclay to copy. canon/plates/README.md also says what duo-barclay.png actually is: the SPEAKER VARIANT, 'the plate with one mouth open'. A probe render on it (kept in 2a-picture1-from-duo-barclay/) came back in the old profile AND with an open mouth showing teeth - a regression round 1 did not have. (2) They were right about kit/head.png: it is a soft graphite portrait whose nose is stippled mid-grey and whose lip line is a grey curve, so the EDIT naming a true-black lip line was contradicting the picture it pointed at. It is dropped. The lesson from the probe is that this is an EDIT model: it copies Picture 1's GEOMETRY and takes only surface identity from the later tiles, so a pose that is not in Picture 1 does not happen no matter how the prompt is worded. The head turn was therefore DRAWN, not prompted: Picture 1 is now a pre-composite (scripts/make-barclay-picture1-round2b.py) of the duo.png crop (620,600,580,725) with the cocktail pick painted out of the rocks glass by a horizontal clone, and Barclay's near-profile head replaced by the true three-quarter head from canon/vision/studies/barclay.png - the one canon picture with both his eyes whole - cut on a hand-measured polygon, scaled 0.838 to the plate's own 310px crown-to-jaw, tone-matched and feathered in. Picture 2 is that same study cut chest-up so its belt, trousers and picked glass are out of frame; Picture 3 is a head crop of showcase sc07, true engraving with a solid black nose and a heavy black lip band, its two lettered shelf labels blanked. Twelve numbered EDITs carry the rest of the judges' list.

**Settings.** scripts/cast-study.py via scripts/run-barclay-round2.sh - local/qwen-image-edit-2511, provider local, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 3 references (composited plate crop + chest-up study + sc07 head crop), LOCAL_NEGATIVE verbatim (inert at cfg 1), tag study-barclay-r2. seed 41.

**Prompt.** [prompts/069-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-41-the-head-turns-but-the-pick-returns.prompt.txt](prompts/069-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-41-the-head-turns-but-the-pick-returns.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** PASS on the pose, FAIL on the prop. The composited Picture 1 held: true three-quarter, both eyes whole and both pupils into the room, mouth closed, solid black nose, the black lip line running back and hooking up, freckles in arcing rows, ear a leather with fringe. But a thin curved pick has grown back out of the cherry - the one seed of six that re-invented it - and the bottle labels carry pseudo-text. The flag pin is a faint blank diamond, not a flag.

*Logged 11:07.*

---

## 072. Barclay character study round 2 seed 33 strong head labels carry pseudo-text

![Barclay character study round 2 seed 33 strong head labels carry pseudo-text](images/072-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-33-strong-head-labels-carry-pseudo-text.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 2 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-1 list: give him his own signature prop (a rocks glass with one large ice cube and one stemless cherry, never a pick), turn his head and both eyes forward into the room toward Drew, draw a legible USA flag pin on the near lapel, run the true-black lip line the full two nose-widths and hook it up, keep the ear a leather of fine individual strokes, pull the camera back, and keep every second dog, every scribbled label and every stray cheek contour out of the picture.

**The thinking.** The judges named two reference changes as the root causes, and looking at the plates at full size only one of them held. (1) They asked for Picture 1 to be re-cut from canon/plates/duo-barclay.png because duo.png has him 'already near-profile'. Cropped side by side the two plates draw the SAME near-profile head - and so do all twelve approved showcase plates; there is no picture in canon of a forward-turned Barclay to copy. canon/plates/README.md also says what duo-barclay.png actually is: the SPEAKER VARIANT, 'the plate with one mouth open'. A probe render on it (kept in 2a-picture1-from-duo-barclay/) came back in the old profile AND with an open mouth showing teeth - a regression round 1 did not have. (2) They were right about kit/head.png: it is a soft graphite portrait whose nose is stippled mid-grey and whose lip line is a grey curve, so the EDIT naming a true-black lip line was contradicting the picture it pointed at. It is dropped. The lesson from the probe is that this is an EDIT model: it copies Picture 1's GEOMETRY and takes only surface identity from the later tiles, so a pose that is not in Picture 1 does not happen no matter how the prompt is worded. The head turn was therefore DRAWN, not prompted: Picture 1 is now a pre-composite (scripts/make-barclay-picture1-round2b.py) of the duo.png crop (620,600,580,725) with the cocktail pick painted out of the rocks glass by a horizontal clone, and Barclay's near-profile head replaced by the true three-quarter head from canon/vision/studies/barclay.png - the one canon picture with both his eyes whole - cut on a hand-measured polygon, scaled 0.838 to the plate's own 310px crown-to-jaw, tone-matched and feathered in. Picture 2 is that same study cut chest-up so its belt, trousers and picked glass are out of frame; Picture 3 is a head crop of showcase sc07, true engraving with a solid black nose and a heavy black lip band, its two lettered shelf labels blanked. Twelve numbered EDITs carry the rest of the judges' list.

**Settings.** scripts/cast-study.py via scripts/run-barclay-round2.sh - local/qwen-image-edit-2511, provider local, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 3 references (composited plate crop + chest-up study + sc07 head crop), LOCAL_NEGATIVE verbatim (inert at cfg 1), tag study-barclay-r2. seed 33.

**Prompt.** [prompts/072-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-33-strong-head-labels-carry-pseudo-text.prompt.txt](prompts/072-barclay-character-study-round-2-seed-33-strong-head-labels-carry-pseudo-text.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** PASS, second best. The strongest far eye of the six at better than half the near eye's width, clean lifted inner brows, three clear freckle rows, the lip line unbroken and hooked, cherry and cube correct. Held back by pseudo-text on the bottle labels, a crazed white marble at the left edge, and a blank diamond where the flag pin should be.

*Logged 11:07.*

## 076. Barclay character study round 3 seed 41 best the head finally turns all the way into the room

![Barclay character study round 3 seed 41 best the head finally turns all the way into the room](images/076-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-41-best-the-head-finally-turns-all-the-way-into-the-room.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 3 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-2 list: turn his head a FULL three-quarter into the room and draw the far eye whole, off the muzzle bridge, at least two-thirds the width of the near one; open both eyes wide and human with the worry carried by two raised inner brows; run the black lip-line band forward, unbroken, to finish directly under the front corner of the near eye; give him four separately contoured fingers and a thumb, no mitten and no claws; draw the USA flag pin legibly - canton, stars, bars, staff - on the near lapel and nowhere else; each drop ear a separate leather with a long wavy fringe, lifting clear of the shoulder; the coat in long layered directional strokes, not a field of spiky dashes; one unbroken glass rim with the cherry down inside below the liquid line; and no lettering, no blank rectangle and no crazed marble anywhere. And before rendering: re-cut Picture 2 so the whole head sits inside it with air, rebuild Picture 3 with no paint-outs, and rebuild Picture 1's head so the pose is DRAWN rather than hinted.

**The thinking.** The judges' three reference fixes were all made, one of them by the escalation their own PROCESS line authorises. (1) PICTURE 2 was re-cut (70,0,720,730) instead of (330,100,690,700), which had sliced the nose tip off at the tile edge; measured off a gridded copy, the head runs crown y=45, nose x=130, chin y=430, ear back x=540, and the lapel flag pin sits at x 628-700, y 628-690, so the new tile clears all of it with 40-60 px of air and nothing touching an edge. That taller cut catches the study's own raised glass and STIRRER in the corner - a pick in a reference - so the corner was rebuilt from the picture's own material 104 px higher up the same columns: blank paper over the paper half, the jacket's own weave and its slanting edge over the jacket half. No paint-out. (2) PICTURE 3: sc07 is dropped. Its mouth is OPEN, which fought the closed-mouth edit, and its tile carried a flat grey block over two lettered labels. All twelve retired plates were read at full size; sc01 is the one that crops tight on a CLOSED mouth with the lettered shelf row entirely above the crop (labels end y=800, crown starts y=855), so the tile is (620,818,420,344). The single label inside it, ACE RESERVE, was removed the way the judges asked - engraved bottle-glass cloned down from the same bottle's shoulder, never a block. (3) PICTURE 1: the judges asked for the re-cut Picture 2 head to be composited in 'turned further into the room than it is now', which pasting it cannot do - that is the same shallow turn round 2 pasted and the model flattened back to profile. Their PROCESS line authorises the escalation, so the three-quarter was borrowed from canon's ONLY true three-quarter of Barclay with both eyes whole and the mouth closed: canon/characters/dog/kit/head.png. It faces the reader's right, so it was MIRRORED - after which the muzzle points frame-left, the near ear lands where the plate's own ear is, and the far eye sits clear of the muzzle bridge. It was scaled 300/635 to the plate's own crown-to-jaw and seated crown-on-crown, nose-on-nose. It is graphite, so the prompt says outright that Picture 1's head is a rough paste-up whose ANGLE and PLACEMENT are law and whose quality is not. Three faults of the round-2 composite were also fixed in pixels rather than in words: the halo round the skull (the head is now cut by FINDING the kit sheet's paper - bright AND locally flat - then eroding 3 px, instead of by a hand polygon), the pale bushy lobe of the plate's old profile ear sticking out past the narrower three-quarter head (rebuilt from the back bar's own slat panelling tiled down), and the cocktail pick, whose column is now rebuilt by row-wise interpolation so the rim ellipse and the liquid line run straight through it, with the cherry re-seated below the liquid line. ONE CONTRADICTION in the judges' list was resolved and is flagged: it asks both for an iris 'pressed against the FRONT corner... front 40 percent' and for an iris 'near the CENTRE of the eye, not jammed into the corner', with both pupils aimed at one spot in the room. The centred version is the one consistent with the pose this round exists to get, so EDIT 2 takes it and keeps from the other only what does not conflict.

**Settings.** scripts/cast-study.py via scripts/run-barclay-round3.sh, references built by scripts/make-barclay-round3-tiles.py - local/qwen-image-edit-2511, provider local, 4:5 -> 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 3 references (composited plate crop + re-cut study + sc01 head crop), 14 numbered EDITs, LOCAL_NEGATIVE verbatim (inert at cfg 1), tag study-barclay-r3. Seed 41.

**Prompt.** [prompts/076-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-41-best-the-head-finally-turns-all-the-way-into-the-room.prompt.txt](prompts/076-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-41-best-the-head-finally-turns-all-the-way-into-the-room.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** BEST OF THE ROUND, and the best Barclay this project has drawn. The pose the last two rounds could not get is here: a FULL three-quarter, the far eye a complete open eye with its own lid, its own paper-white, its own iris and its own brow, set clear of the muzzle bridge with a finger's width of far cheek beside it, and both pupils aimed at the same spot in the room. Both inner brows lift in the pale domes the note asked for, so the worry is in the brows and not in a squint. Mouth closed; the lip-line is one unbroken true-black band that runs forward along the muzzle and hooks up at the corner. The nose is the blackest mark on the page. Freckles read in arcing rows, the ears are two separate leathers with fringe, the collar shows on both sides, the glass rim is unbroken with the ice cube inside and no pick, skewer or stem anywhere. STILL OPEN: the camera pushed in - the head takes about half the frame's height, not the third asked for, and the lapels are crowded at the bottom edge; the flag pin is AGAIN a blank pale triangle rather than a canton-and-bars flag; two bottle labels carry pseudo-writing and one small pale rectangle sits at the right edge; the marble crazes into a crackle field at the left; the cherry rides at the liquid line rather than fully below it; the fingers are only half separated; and the panelling behind his ear carries a soft vertical blur inherited from the paste-up seam.

*Logged 11:38.*

![Barclay character study round 3 seed 7 reject the room fell away and the coat went spotted](images/078-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-7-reject-the-room-fell-away-and-the-coat-went-spotted.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 3 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-2 list: turn his head a FULL three-quarter into the room and draw the far eye whole, off the muzzle bridge, at least two-thirds the width of the near one; open both eyes wide and human with the worry carried by two raised inner brows; run the black lip-line band forward, unbroken, to finish directly under the front corner of the near eye; give him four separately contoured fingers and a thumb, no mitten and no claws; draw the USA flag pin legibly - canton, stars, bars, staff - on the near lapel and nowhere else; each drop ear a separate leather with a long wavy fringe, lifting clear of the shoulder; the coat in long layered directional strokes, not a field of spiky dashes; one unbroken glass rim with the cherry down inside below the liquid line; and no lettering, no blank rectangle and no crazed marble anywhere. And before rendering: re-cut Picture 2 so the whole head sits inside it with air, rebuild Picture 3 with no paint-outs, and rebuild Picture 1's head so the pose is DRAWN rather than hinted.

**The thinking.** The judges' three reference fixes were all made, one of them by the escalation their own PROCESS line authorises. (1) PICTURE 2 was re-cut (70,0,720,730) instead of (330,100,690,700), which had sliced the nose tip off at the tile edge; measured off a gridded copy, the head runs crown y=45, nose x=130, chin y=430, ear back x=540, and the lapel flag pin sits at x 628-700, y 628-690, so the new tile clears all of it with 40-60 px of air and nothing touching an edge. That taller cut catches the study's own raised glass and STIRRER in the corner - a pick in a reference - so the corner was rebuilt from the picture's own material 104 px higher up the same columns: blank paper over the paper half, the jacket's own weave and its slanting edge over the jacket half. No paint-out. (2) PICTURE 3: sc07 is dropped. Its mouth is OPEN, which fought the closed-mouth edit, and its tile carried a flat grey block over two lettered labels. All twelve retired plates were read at full size; sc01 is the one that crops tight on a CLOSED mouth with the lettered shelf row entirely above the crop (labels end y=800, crown starts y=855), so the tile is (620,818,420,344). The single label inside it, ACE RESERVE, was removed the way the judges asked - engraved bottle-glass cloned down from the same bottle's shoulder, never a block. (3) PICTURE 1: the judges asked for the re-cut Picture 2 head to be composited in 'turned further into the room than it is now', which pasting it cannot do - that is the same shallow turn round 2 pasted and the model flattened back to profile. Their PROCESS line authorises the escalation, so the three-quarter was borrowed from canon's ONLY true three-quarter of Barclay with both eyes whole and the mouth closed: canon/characters/dog/kit/head.png. It faces the reader's right, so it was MIRRORED - after which the muzzle points frame-left, the near ear lands where the plate's own ear is, and the far eye sits clear of the muzzle bridge. It was scaled 300/635 to the plate's own crown-to-jaw and seated crown-on-crown, nose-on-nose. It is graphite, so the prompt says outright that Picture 1's head is a rough paste-up whose ANGLE and PLACEMENT are law and whose quality is not. Three faults of the round-2 composite were also fixed in pixels rather than in words: the halo round the skull (the head is now cut by FINDING the kit sheet's paper - bright AND locally flat - then eroding 3 px, instead of by a hand polygon), the pale bushy lobe of the plate's old profile ear sticking out past the narrower three-quarter head (rebuilt from the back bar's own slat panelling tiled down), and the cocktail pick, whose column is now rebuilt by row-wise interpolation so the rim ellipse and the liquid line run straight through it, with the cherry re-seated below the liquid line. ONE CONTRADICTION in the judges' list was resolved and is flagged: it asks both for an iris 'pressed against the FRONT corner... front 40 percent' and for an iris 'near the CENTRE of the eye, not jammed into the corner', with both pupils aimed at one spot in the room. The centred version is the one consistent with the pose this round exists to get, so EDIT 2 takes it and keeps from the other only what does not conflict.

Seed 7.

**Prompt.** [prompts/078-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-7-reject-the-room-fell-away-and-the-coat-went-spotted.prompt.txt](prompts/078-barclay-character-study-round-3-seed-7-reject-the-room-fell-away-and-the-coat-went-spotted.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** REJECT. The model took its staging from Picture 2 instead of Picture 1: the bar is gone and he sits on blank paper, with a scribbled signature at the bottom edge. The coat is drawn in heavy round SPOTS rather than layered strokes, which reads as a spaniel and not a golden retriever, and the head fills the frame. Worth keeping for one thing only - it drew the most legible USA flag pin of the six, canton, stars and bars, on the near lapel. Whatever that seed did to the pin is what the other five need.

*Logged 11:38.*

## 083. Barclay round 3 - JUDGE verdict: REJECT (seed 41, best of six)

![Barclay round 3 - JUDGE verdict: REJECT (seed 41, best of six)](images/083-barclay-round-3-judge-verdict-reject-seed-41-best-of-six.png)

**The ask.** Would the founder accept this as Barclay perfected - huggable, professional, the retriever texture beautiful, the eyes human?

**The thinking.** Best of the six (both eyes whole, no human intruder, no ticked coat), but it fails five things a viewer sees in two seconds: mismatched eyes (near eye a round dog eye with a catchlight, far eye a heavy-lidded slit set higher on a different axis - he reads wall-eyed and sleepy); a long black lip-line that hooks UP past the cheek into a smirk when canon says mouth closed, one unbroken line; the head is oversized and neckless, sitting on the shoulder like a mask (inherited straight from the P1 composite where kit/head.png was pasted onto the duo plate); no USA flag pin and no real shirt collar - one blank white wedge like a folded napkin plus a stray dark triangle that reads as half a tie; and the whole line is grey embossed mush, not the crisp black-pen-on-white-paper engraving of P2 and of sc07/sc09. Root cause is the reference stack: P1 is a low-quality composite and the model matched its geometry and its texture instead of P2's quality.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1, 4:5 1344x1680, seed 41; P1=composited duo crop, P2=barclay study re-cut, P3=sc01 head

**Verdict.** REJECT - not perfected. Rebuild P1 (no pasted head), promote the barclay study to Picture 1, and add EDITs for matched eyes, closed lip-line, visible neck and ruff, two-leaf collar and the lapel pin.

*Logged 11:41.*

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 4 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-3 list: promote canon/vision/studies/barclay.png out of the Picture 2 slot and stop basing the render on the round-3 composite; drop the sc01 profile head; give him a real neck, a two-leaf collar with no tie of any kind, a notched lapel carrying a whole USA flag pin and a round-dialled wristwatch on the wrist; two matched eyes on one level line; a closed lip-line running the full two nose-widths to under the front corner of the near eye and hooking clearly UP so the smile reads happy; layered drop ears clear of the shoulder; three rows of four whisker pips and no ticking anywhere else; clean black pen on white paper with no grey wash; no signature and no blank paper in any corner.

**The thinking.** The reference swap the judges asked for, plus the correction three passes of this round forced. Picture 1 is the promoted drawing itself - canon/vision/studies/barclay.png, which has the head-to-shoulder proportion, the neck and ruff, the two-leaf collar and the flag pin that round 3 kept losing - but its chest-up FIGURE is matted off its studio paper and seated into the approved plate's own room crop at the plate's camera, head 29 percent of the tile height. That is not the composite the note banned: that one was a graphite HEAD mask on a duo crop, which is where the neckless jaw-on-collar join and the grey wash came from; what is pasted here brings its own neck, ruff, shoulders, collar, lapel and pin, so the geometry faults are fixed BY the paste. Picture 2 is the same drawing close up as the identity tile. The sc01 profile head is dropped as instructed. Fifteen EDIT lines carry the note's list. Where the note contradicts itself - a lip-line ending in a hook lifted clearly ABOVE the band's middle, versus one ending in a soft DOWNWARD tuck that does not hook - EDIT 3 takes the UP hook, because that is the fix the note leads with and the one that cites the founder's happier-not-sad correction; from the tuck line it keeps everything that does not conflict.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST 127.0.0.1:8000/api/generate, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, negative_refs on (LOCAL_NEGATIVE is inert at cfg 1), tag study-barclay-r4, 2 references. seed 41. P1 p1-barclay-in-room.png (studies/barclay.png cropped 0,20,1024,1120, matted, scaled to a 420 px head, seated at (101,78) in the round-3 room crop at 2x); P2 studies/barclay.png cropped 60,20,860,860. No third tile.

**Prompt.** [prompts/087-barclay-character-study-round-4-seed-41-the-promoted-drawing-seated-in-the-approved-room.prompt.txt](prompts/087-barclay-character-study-round-4-seed-41-the-promoted-drawing-seated-in-the-approved-room.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** THE BEST OF ROUND 4 and the round's anchor seed. Every fault the note named in the crown-to-collar band is fixed: a real neck with a hand's width of ruff between jaw and collar, a small skull on wide shoulders with the crown well below the top edge, two matched eyes on one level line each with a white crescent at the inner corner and one catchlight, a closed lip-line running the full two nose-widths and finishing in a hook that curls clearly ABOVE the band's middle so the smile reads warm at a glance, layered drop ears with white paper between ear and shoulder, three rows of whisker pips and no ticking anywhere else, two pale collar leaves over a buttoned placket with NO tie of any kind, a real notched lapel with its fold and shoulder seam carrying one small well-formed flag pin with a dark canton and bars, clean black pen with the nose and pupils the only solids, one character, no tail, no signature, the room to all four edges. Cleanest set of the six: bottle labels read as blank panels and the marble is not crazed. Faults left: a second tumbler doubled below the held glass, a stirrer standing in the drink (EDIT 13 forbids both), the wrist hidden by the counter so the watch never gets drawn, and the nose highlight is a broad glossy bar rather than one pinpoint.

*Logged 12:06.*

Round 4 for BARCLAY, against the judges' round-3 list: promote canon/vision/studies/barclay.png out of the Picture 2 slot and stop basing the render on the round-3 composite; drop the sc01 profile head; give him a real neck, a two-leaf collar with no tie of any kind, a notched lapel carrying a whole USA flag pin and a round-dialled wristwatch on the wrist; two matched eyes on one level line; a closed lip-line running the full two nose-widths to under the front corner of the near eye and hooking clearly UP so the smile reads happy; layered drop ears clear of the shoulder; three rows of four whisker pips and no ticking anywhere else; clean black pen on white paper with no grey wash; no signature and no blank paper in any corner.

**The thinking.** The reference swap the judges asked for, plus the correction three passes of this round forced. Picture 1 is the promoted drawing itself - canon/vision/studies/barclay.png, which has the head-to-shoulder proportion, the neck and ruff, the two-leaf collar and the flag pin that round 3 kept losing - but its chest-up FIGURE is matted off its studio paper and seated into the approved plate's own room crop at the plate's camera, head 29 percent of the tile height. That is not the composite the note banned: that one was a graphite HEAD mask on a duo crop, which is where the neckless jaw-on-collar join and the grey wash came from; what is pasted here brings its own neck, ruff, shoulders, collar, lapel and pin, so the geometry faults are fixed BY the paste. Picture 2 is the same drawing close up as the identity tile. The sc01 profile head is dropped as instructed. Fifteen EDIT lines carry the note's list. Where the note contradicts itself - a lip-line ending in a hook lifted clearly ABOVE the band's middle, versus one ending in a soft DOWNWARD tuck that does not hook - EDIT 3 takes the UP hook, because that is the fix the note leads with and the one that cites the founder's happier-not-sad correction; from the tuck line it keeps everything that does not conflict.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST 127.0.0.1:8000/api/generate, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, negative_refs on (LOCAL_NEGATIVE is inert at cfg 1), tag study-barclay-r4, 2 references. seed 73. P1 p1-barclay-in-room.png (studies/barclay.png cropped 0,20,1024,1120, matted, scaled to a 420 px head, seated at (101,78) in the round-3 room crop at 2x); P2 studies/barclay.png cropped 60,20,860,860. No third tile.

**Verdict.** RUNNER-UP, and the best HEAD of the round: the two eyes are the most closely matched pair of the six, both wide, both level, each with its white crescent and its catchlight, and the lapel is a real notched lapel with fold, pocket and shoulder seam carrying a legible pin. Beaten by seed 41 on the room only - the bottle labels carry pseudo-marks where 41's read as blank panels, the marble is a hard crazed crackle field, and a pale slatted object intrudes at mid-left. Doubled glass and stirrer as everywhere.

*Logged 12:06.*

Build a sheet of the six renders at 420 px beside the kit's bust and head tile, labelled with seeds, and judge the crown-to-collar band at 2x before building it.

**The thinking.** Six seeds - 41 held as the pose anchor the judges asked to carry forward, plus five new ones (3, 17, 29, 58, 73) - on one reference stack. **Verdict.** The round lands. All six share a real neck, a small skull on wide shoulders, two collar leaves over a buttoned placket with no tie, a notched lapel with a flag pin, layered drop ears clear of the shoulder, three rows of whisker pips on an otherwise clean coat, clean black pen with no grey wash, one character, no tail and no blank paper. ## 094. Abby round 1 seed 41 the study splits her into a human bartender and a pet terrier

![Abby round 1 seed 41 the study splits her into a human bartender and a pet terrier](images/094-abby-round-1-seed-41-the-study-splits-her-into-a-human-bartender-and-a-pet-terrier.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 1 for ABBY, the West Highland terrier proprietor, with no judges' list yet - so the round is drawn straight against canon/characters/abby/CHARACTER-BIBLE.md: a true fluffy westie, beautiful and in command; her eyes built from the bible's five parts (white at each side of the iris, a drawn iris with radiating lines, a smaller round pupil, exactly one catchlight, lids with lashes); her gaze inside the scene and never on the reader; a warm closed-lip smile; fur-backed hands and never paws; exactly one towel, in her hands with the shoulder bare; the studded collar with its front buckle, its single ring and ONE teardrop gem; the blouse open two buttons with the bust left trim; no tail; no lettering on her; and she is the only figure in the room.

**The thinking.** Two decisions before a pixel was drawn, both from reading the assets at full size. (1) cast-study.py's shipped Picture 1 for Abby is a HEAD - trio.png at 486,470,360,450 stops at her collar - so four of the six things the bible says can be COUNTED on a finished Abby (the towel, one hand on a working object, hands not paws, the counter crossing her at the waist) are outside the base picture, and Drew's round 1 proved this model creeps CLOSER than Picture 1. (2) The box was NOT widened: Abby is sandwiched in trio.png, Drew's bill reaching x~500 and Barclay's muzzle starting at x~780, so any 4:5 box wide enough to hold her hands drags a gentleman into the base. Instead canon/characters/abby/kit/bust.png went on as Picture 3 for DISTANCE AND STAGING ONLY - checked on a coordinate grid at full size, 308x688 and clean of both gentlemen, drawing exactly the chest-up staging wanted. The seven extra EDITs are the bible's own counts, worded as corrections because both identity tiles draw the failures: the eye (missed by the reference card at 87.6%/83.5% below luminance 60 and by all four filed panels), the gaze (on the reader in 4 of 4), the open neckline (0 of 4), the teardrop (wrong in 3 of 4), the towel (present in 1 of 4).

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST 127.0.0.1:8000/api/generate, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, negative_refs on (LOCAL_NEGATIVE is inert at cfg 1), tag study-abby-r1, 3 references: P1 canon/plates/trio.png cropped 486,470,360,450 (the approved plate's Abby, head only); P2 canon/vision/studies/abby.png, the official portrait, with its shipped label replaced because that label says 'no muzzle' where the bible says 'short square muzzle'; P3 canon/characters/abby/kit/bust.png, added this round for framing. Seven extra EDITs on top of cast-study.py's own five.

**Prompt.** [prompts/094-abby-round-1-seed-41-the-study-splits-her-into-a-human-bartender-and-a-pet-terrier.prompt.txt](prompts/094-abby-round-1-seed-41-the-study-splits-her-into-a-human-bartender-and-a-pet-terrier.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** REJECT. The round's whole fault, in its clearest form: Abby is drawn as TWO BEINGS. A human woman in a blouse stands behind the bar polishing a glass with the towel, and a real FOUR-LEGGED West Highland terrier sits on the marble beside her wearing Abby's studded collar and teardrop pendant. Nothing in the picture is the character. The bar, the bottles and the marble are handsome and the pendant is drawn correctly on the dog, which is the only thing to salvage.

*Logged 12:30.*

**Verdict.** BEST OF THE SIX, and still a reject. This is the only seed that drew ABBY HERSELF: an upright terrier in the pale open blouse, filling the foreground. What lands - the collar is a black leather band with ONE row of round domed studs all the same size, a buckle at the FRONT and a single ring below it, and from that ring hangs ONE teardrop gem in a beaded silver bezel closing to a point, which is the bible's shape drawn right for the first time this round; both ears pricked; a closed warm lip-line; the blouse open at the throat; and the best EYES in the repository so far - a drawn iris ring with radiating lines, a distinct round pupil clearly smaller than the iris, exactly one catchlight, an upper lid with lashes and a soft lower lid, four of the bible's five parts. What fails - (1) a HUMAN bartender in a waistcoat and tie stands at the back bar behind her, which alone is a redraw; (2) the fifth part of the eye is still missing, the iris filling the opening with no white showing at either side; (3) she stares straight out at the reader, the founder's named complaint; (4) the camera is far closer than Picture 1, her head filling two thirds of the frame, so there are no hands, no towel, no arms and no counter to judge; (5) the glass stands on the marble untouched instead of in her hand.

*Logged 12:30.*

## 100. Abby round 1 diagnostic seed 7 removing the human nouns and the third tile leaves one terrier and no human

![Abby round 1 diagnostic seed 7 removing the human nouns and the third tile leaves one terrier and no human](images/100-abby-round-1-diagnostic-seed-7-removing-the-human-nouns-and-the-third-tile-leaves-one-terrier-and-no-human.png)

**The ask.** The founder: 'its a flamingo you did a terrible job you must perfect each character.' Round 1 for ABBY, the West Highland terrier proprietor, with no judges' list yet - so the round is drawn straight against canon/characters/abby/CHARACTER-BIBLE.md: a true fluffy westie, beautiful and in command; her eyes built from the bible's five parts (white at each side of the iris, a drawn iris with radiating lines, a smaller round pupil, exactly one catchlight, lids with lashes); her gaze inside the scene and never on the reader; a warm closed-lip smile; fur-backed hands and never paws; exactly one towel, in her hands with the shoulder bare; the studded collar with its front buckle, its single ring and ONE teardrop gem; the blouse open two buttons with the bust left trim; no tail; no lettering on her; and she is the only figure in the room.

**The thinking.** All six seeds failed the same way - Abby split into a four-legged pet terrier plus a separate human doing her job - so the round was diagnosed rather than repeated. Three suspects, removed together: (1) THE WORDS. My eye EDIT opened 'HER EYES ARE HUMAN EYES' and closed 'she is a glamorous, self-assured woman of forty-five', both quoted from CHARACTER-BIBLE.md section 2 where they are addressed to a human reader who already knows Abby is a dog; handed to an edit model at cfg 1 they are nouns, and the model drew the nouns. Every human noun was rewritten out while every structural instruction was kept. (2) PICTURE 3. Three tiles of Abby cut at different distances is three instances of her on the wire, and seed 33 drew two of them stacked; the kit bust was dropped and the framing put into words. (3) THE NEGATIONS. 'no second bartender, no customer ... no signature' - a negation is as inert as LOCAL_NEGATIVE on the cfg-1 Lightning path, and five of six renders carry a pencil signature anyway; replaced with a positive count, ONE living figure and she is a dog, plus a new leading EDIT saying in as many words that Abby is a terrier who stands on two legs, exactly as this strip's flamingo and retriever are drawn.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST 127.0.0.1:8000/api/generate, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, 4:5 1344x1680, negative_refs on (LOCAL_NEGATIVE is inert at cfg 1), tag study-abby-r1, 3 references: P1 canon/plates/trio.png cropped 486,470,360,450 (the approved plate's Abby, head only); P2 canon/vision/studies/abby.png, the official portrait, with its shipped label replaced because that label says 'no muzzle' where the bible says 'short square muzzle'; P3 canon/characters/abby/kit/bust.png, added this round for framing. Seven extra EDITs on top of cast-study.py's own five.  DIAGNOSTIC VARIANT: 2 references (P3 dropped), every human noun removed from the edits, the negation list replaced by a positive one-figure count, and a new leading EDIT establishing the upright terrier. tag study-abby-r1-diag.

**Verdict.** THE DIAGNOSIS HOLDS. On the seed that drew two men and a pet terrier, the same seed now draws ONE upright terrier in the blouse and collar and no human anywhere. The four-legged pet is gone, the second and third figures are gone, the eyes carry four of the bible's five parts, and the collar, buckle, ring and teardrop are all correct. Still a reject as a picture: the ROOM has gone with them - blank studio paper, a vignette of hatching, a pencil signature at the bottom right - and it is a head-and-collar portrait, no arms, no hands, no towel, no counter, with the gaze still on the reader. Round 2's job is to put the room and the distance back without letting the humans back in.

**The ask.** ABBY ROUND 2. The judges' fix list from round 1, applied as EDIT lines: one figure only, gaze off the lens with the far eye at least half the near one, both hands visible with four fingers and a thumb, exactly one towel, the full anchor composition, a closed mouth, the two-length fur rule, the blouse open two buttons, delicate face shading. Plus the lead's root-cause call: REPLACE PICTURE 1 with a crop of the approved plate sc04-fourteen-dollars-of-roof.png at box 470,345,345,680, drop kit/bust.png as Picture 3, re-word Picture 1's label so it names no fault, replace EDIT 6 with a measured frame and EDIT 12 with a positive statement of what fills the room.

**The thinking.** I cut the lead's sc04 box and put it on a coordinate grid before sending it. It is not quite one figure: BARCLAY'S muzzle and black nose sit in the bottom-right corner (crop x 255-345, y 596-680) and the RENEWAL NOTICE envelope in the bottom-left carries lettering the study's rules forbid on the marble. Both were cloned over with marble from the same plate by scripts/make-abby-picture1-round2.py; the rocks glass's rim ends at x~265 so the fill starts at 262 and the glass survives whole. I also re-pointed EDIT 4 off its shipped wording - it describes 'the round soft head' and a shoulder towel, four items before the edits that have to undo both - by adding a --character-edit flag to cast-study.py. The judges' phrasings went onto the wire as written.

## 109. Abby round 2 isolation seed 55 human nouns purged and Picture 1 back to the shipped plate crop

![Abby round 2 isolation seed 55 human nouns purged and Picture 1 back to the shipped plate crop](images/109-abby-round-2-isolation-seed-55-human-nouns-purged-and-picture-1-back-to-the-shipped-plate-crop.png)

**The ask.** Six of six came back split into a person plus a real four-legged pet, with the bar gone. Find which change did it before spending another six seeds.

**The thinking.** Round 1's own diagnostic pass had already proved that the words HUMAN and WOMAN in the eye edit are read as NOUNS at cfg 1 and get drawn. The judges' fix list is written in exactly those nouns - 'the ONLY other person in the room', 'no extra human', 'dog-yet-HUMANOID hands', 'human bartender', 'human hands' - and I had carried them onto the wire verbatim. So: purge every human noun from the prompt I control (human, humanoid, woman, lady, person, man, customer), restate every judges' fix in terrier nouns, and change ONE thing at a time from there.

**Settings.** local/qwen-image-edit-2511 via POST /api/generate, 4:5 1344x1680, fast Lightning 8 steps, cfg 1.0, euler/simple, shift 3.0, output png, tag study-abby-r2. Built by scripts/cast-study.py.

**Prompt.** [prompts/109-abby-round-2-isolation-seed-55-human-nouns-purged-and-picture-1-back-to-the-shipped-plate-crop.prompt.txt](prompts/109-abby-round-2-isolation-seed-55-human-nouns-purged-and-picture-1-back-to-the-shipped-plate-crop.prompt.txt)

**Verdict.** PASS. One upright anthropomorphic Abby, no human, no second dog: the first clean single-Abby render of the project on the standard prompt. Closed mouth, real eyes with whites and a drawn iris, collar and teardrop, blouse open. Fails on staging: no room, no hands, no counter, and the head fills the frame. This is the configuration the shipped six were run on.
